======================================================================
INDIAN HISTORY KNOWLEDGE BASE
======================================================================

Entry 1
Category: Ancient India
Concept: Indian History
Definition: The study of the political, cultural and social development of the Indian subcontinent.
Key Points:
- Important topic in Indian history.
- Frequently asked in competitive examinations.
- Significant for understanding India's cultural and political development.
Example:
Indian History is an important topic in Indian history.
Related Concepts:
Ancient India, Medieval India, Freedom Movement, Constitution, Culture

Entry 2
Category: Ancient India
Concept: Indus Valley Civilization
Definition: One of the world's earliest urban civilizations, flourishing around 2600–1900 BCE.
Key Points:
- Important topic in Indian history.
- Frequently asked in competitive examinations.
- Significant for understanding India's cultural and political development.
Example:
Known for advanced urban planning and drainage systems.
Related Concepts:
Ancient India, Medieval India, Freedom Movement, Constitution, Culture
